Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the linear_algebra structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 308. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 563 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 308 + O(1/n^563). Summing over all modes and applying the linear_algebra convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
